Local binary search details:
Given h binary length n with sum n/2. compute_all_lags c (scaled by dx). For a swap between i (currently 1, j 0) set i->0, j->1. Need update c for all lags r. Let's derive direct formula with arrays scaled? Maybe work with unnormalized counts S_r = ∑ h_i(1-h_{i+r}) for valid indices. c = S_r dx. Objective = max S_r * dx. dx constant; minimize max S_r integer. For binary, S_r integer. We can maintain counts int.

For swap: old h_i=1, h_j=0. New: h'_i=0,h'_j=1.
For each lag r:
S_r = ∑_{i valid} h_i (1-h_{i+r}). For fixed r, contribution depending on positions in source index set A_r={i, 0≤i<n-|r|} and target i+r.
Change from bit change at position p (0→1 or 1→0). Let's derive for single flip p: new h_p changes δ = +1 (0 to1) or -1 (1 to0). Contribution to S_r from terms where p is source: term p if p in A_r: h_p(1-h_{p+r}) changes δ*(1-h_{p+r}). Where p is target: term p-r if p-r in A_r: h_{p-r}(1-h_p) changes h_{p-r}*(-δ) = -δ h_{p-r}. So ΔS_r(δ) = δ*(1 - h_{p+r}) * [p source] - δ*h_{p-r}*[p target]. (using old values before flip). For two flips, nonlinear interaction if terms involve both positions (i.e., one is source and other target for same term: p and q such that q=p+r or p=q+r). Then above sum using old values double counts? We can compute exact for swap with formula: For each term affected (source or target p or q), recompute old vs new for relevant pairs. Simpler for update O(n): For each r, identify source positions: i=p (if p valid source), i=p-r (if p target), i=q, i=q-r. Up to four terms. For each unique source i, compute old term = old_h[i]*(1-old_h[i+r]); new term = new_h[i]*(1-new_h[i+r]); Δ += new-old. This is O(1) per r, total O(n). Good.

We can maintain S array (int or float). For n maybe up to 1000. Swaps: choose random 1/0 pair, compute ΔS all r? Could be expensive but okay for thousands. Need find improving swap: could random search. Better: For each 1 and 0, evaluate if max decreases. O(n^2 * n) impossible. Use heuristic: target active lag r*. For binary, to reduce S_{r*}, we want swap a 1 at source i where target 0 with 0 at source/target? Actually decrease S_r by moving a 1 from a "bad" source to a target where it fills 0? Specifically if h_i=1, h_{i+r}=0 (bad source), and there is j with h_j=0, h_{j+r}=1 (bad target? If move 1 to j, pair (i,i+r) no longer bad (source 0) and pair (j,j+r) no longer bad (target 1) if j+r=1? Let's use local improvement for active lag: choose bad source i (h_i=1,h_{i+m}=0) and bad target j (h_j=0,h_{j+m}=1) such that moving 1 from i to j reduces S_m by 2 if no other interactions. Own c compute efficiently:
def compute_counts(h): n=len(h); S = np.empty(2n-1); for r in range(1,n): pos = sum(h[:n-r]*(1-h[r:])); neg = sum(h[r:]* (1-h[:n-r])); S[n-1+r]=pos; S[n-1-r]=neg; S[n-1]=np.sum(h*(1-h)). Return S.

Great. Then delta evaluation O(1) per lag using single flip terms, no source candidates. Let's write:
def binary_lag_delta(h, r, p, q, n): old hp=h[p], hq=h[q] assume 1/0.
 d=abs(r); idx=n-1+r.
 delta=0
 if r>=0:
   if p < n-r: delta -= (1 - h[p+r])  # p source removal? Wait formula for p δ=-1: source Δ = -(1 - h_target) = - (1 - h[p+r])
   if p >= r: delta += h[p-r] # p target creation: +h[p-r]
   if q < n-r: delta += (1 - h[q+r]) # q source creation
   if q >= r: delta -= h[q-r] # q target removal
 else:
   d=-r
   # source i in [d,n-1], target i-d
   if p >= d: delta -= (1 - h[p-d]) # p source removal: -(1 - h_target)
   if p < n: # p as target -> source p+d
      if p + d < n: delta += h[p+d]
   if q >= d: delta += (1 - h[q-d])
   if q + d < n: delta -= h[q+d]
 if abs(p-q) == abs(r): delta += 1
 return delta
Check positive r source p: p < n-r. target p: p-r >=0 => p>=r. q source q<n-r, q target q>=r. For negative, d: source if p>=d, target if p+d<n.
Test cases:
- p source, q target, r>0, q=p+r: p<n-r yes; q<p? q>=r yes? q target: delta -= h[q-r]=h[p]=1. p target? p>=r? no. q source? q<n-r no. Delta sum: p source -(1-h[q])=-(1)= -1; q target -h[p]= -1; correction +1 => -1 correct.
- source q target p, q=p-r (p=q+r): p target: p>=r yes +h[p-r]=h[q]=0; q source: q<n-r yes +(1-h[p])=0; correction +1 => +1 correct.
- p and q unrelated: okay.
This is very fast: for each r, up to 8 conditions. We can vectorize over r? Could compute arrays of delta components over all r for a swap using broadcasting. For p,q fixed, we can compute delta array length 2n-1 vectorized:

def compute_counts_bin(h): n; S=np.empty(2n-1); S[n-1]=0 (since binary); for m in 1..n-1: S[n-1+m]=np.sum(h[:n-m] * (1-h[m:])); S[n-1-m]=np.sum(h[m:] * (1-h[:n-m])); return S. For binary, use np.count_nonzero? h[:,None]? Use dot: int((h[:n-m]*(1-h[m:])).sum()).

def binary_hill(h, S, deadline, rng, max_time): We can pass S.
Pseudo:
  n=len(h); dx=2/n; best_c=S.max()*dx; best_h=h.copy(); best_S=S.copy()
  start=time.time(); last_improve=start
  while time < start + max_time - 1:
    max_count=best_S.max()
    # top active indices
    top_idx = np.argsort(best_S)[-top_m:][::-1]; maybe limit where >= max-1
    cand_pairs=set()
    for idx in top_idx[:max_active]:
      r=idx-(n-1)
      # compute A for ones, B for zeros
      ones = np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros = np.where(h==0)[0]
      A = zeros_like ones? compute array len ones
      B = array len zeros
      If r>0:
        m=r
        for idxp,p in enumerate(ones):
          a=0
          if p < n-m: a -= (1 - h[p+m])
          if p >= m: a += h[p-m]
          A[idxp]=a
        for idxq,q in enumerate(zeros):
          b=0
          if q < n-m: b += (1 - h[q+m])
          if q >= m: b -= h[q-m]
          B[idxq]=b
      else m=-r:
        if p >= m: A -= (1 - h[p-m])
        if p + m < n: A += h[p+m]
        if q >= m: B += (1 - h[q-m])
        if q + m < n: B -= h[q-m]
      # scores = A[:,None]+B[None,:] ; correction +1 where abs(ones[:,None]-zeros[None,:])==m
      diff = np.abs(ones[:,None] - zeros[None,:])
      # We want negative scores. Maybe only consider score < 0.
      scores = A[:,None] + B[None,:]
      scores[diff == m] += 1
      # choose pairs with scores <= -1 (or minimum if none)
      # To avoid huge sort each round, find flat indices of minimum or <= -1.
      min_score = scores.min()
      if min_score < 0:
         # get up to num sample flat indices with scores == min? Or <= -1
         flat = np.argwhere(scores <= -1).ravel()
         if len(flat)>max_pairs: flat = rng.choice(flat, max_pairs, replace=False)
         for f in flat:
            i,j = divmod(f, len(zeros)); cand_pairs.add((ones[i], zeros[j]))
    if not cand_pairs:
       # random perturb or restart
       break? do random anneal for some time then restart
    # evaluate candidates exact full delta
    best_local = None
    for p,q in cand_pairs:
       delta = binary_full_delta(h,p,q,n)
       new_S = S + delta
       # since counts integer, max
       nm = new_S.max()
       if nm < best_local or (nm==best_local and maybe): track
    if best and nm < max_count - eps:
       update S += delta; h[p]=0;h[q]=1; last_improve
    else:
       if no improvement for a while: do annealing/random
  return best_h,best_c

project_feasible: same as parent but more exact. Could implement:
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2
  for _ in range(100):
    s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    if diff>0:
      room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      h += diff/tot*room
    else:
      tot=h.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      h += diff/tot*h
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # adjust one element for exact sum
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-14:
     if diff>0:
       idx=np.argmax(1-h)
       add=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
     else:
       idx=np.argmax(h)
       add=max(diff,-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
  # if still diff, spread over many
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-12:
      # distribute over all entries with room
      if diff>0: room=1-h; w=room/room.sum(); h+=diff*w
      else: w=h/h.sum(); h+=diff*w
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  return h
Should be fine.

compute_c5 corr:
def eval_c5_ours(h):
  n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')*dx))

def smooth_max_obj(x,n,alpha,top_idx=None):
  c = np.correlate(x,1-x)* (2/n)
  if top_idx is None: a=c
  else: a=c[top_idx]
  m=a.max(); sh=alpha*(a-m); mx=sh.max()
  if mx>500: return m + (mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx))))/alpha
  return m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh)))/alpha

def c_selected(x, top_idx):
  n=len(x); out=np.empty(len(top_idx)); dx=2/n
  for t, idx in enumerate(top_idx):
    r=idx-(n-1)
    if r>0:
      out[t]=np.sum(x[:n-r]*(1-x[r:]))*dx
    elif r<0:
      m=-r; out[t]=np.sum(x[m:]*(1-x[:n-m]))*dx
    else:
      out[t]=np.sum(x*(1-x))*dx
  return out

Gradient for SLSQP? SLSQP approximates numerically by default if jac not given. That is O(n) extra per eval, bad. We can provide analytic gradient for selected smooth max. Let's derive.
For selected lags c_t. We need gradient of g=soft sum exp(alpha(c-m)). grad g = (1/Z)∑ w_t grad c_t, where w_t=exp(alpha(c_t-m)). For each lag r, gradient of c_r (unscaled) times dx:
For r>0:
  I = ∑_{i=0}^{n-r-1} x_i - x_i x_{i+r}
  grad:
   for i=0..n-r-1: +=1 - x_{i+r}
   for i=r..n-1: -= x_{i-r}
  scale dx.
For r<0, m=-r:
  I = ∑_{j=0}^{n-m-1} x_{j+m}(1-x_j)
  grad:
   for j=0..n-m-1 as target: -= x_{j+m}
   for j=m..n-1 as source: += 1 - x_{j-m}
  scale dx.
For r=0: grad = 1 - 2x * dx? I=∑ x(1-x), derivative 1-2x.
We can implement selected_lag_grad(x, idx) O(n). For smooth objective, active selected.

`compute_counts_own(h)`: h floats but 0/1. Use for all even.
  n=int(len(h)); S=np.zeros(2*n-1)

  for d in range(1,n):
    L=n-d
    S[n-1+d] = float(np.dot(h[:L], 1-h[d:d+L])) # h[:L]*(1-h[d:])
    S[n-1-d] = float(np.dot(h[d:], 1-h[:L]))
  return S

Pseudo:
def binary_hill(h, n, deadline, rng):
  h=h.copy(); S=compute_counts_own(h); best_S=S.copy(); best_h=h.copy(); best_count=S.max()
  start=time.time(); no_improve=0
  while time.time() < deadline - 0.5:
    max_count = S.max()
    idx_max = np.argmax(S)
    # active top
    order = np.argsort(S)[-6:]
    top_idx = [i for i in order if S[i] >= max_count - 1] maybe if none include idx_max
    cand = set()
    ones = np.flatnonzero(h==1); zeros = np.flatnonzero(h==0)
    if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
    for idx in top_idx[:4]:
      r = idx - (n-1)
      # compute A,B
      A = np.empty(ones.size)
      B = np.empty(zeros.size)
      for ip,p in enumerate(ones): ...
      for iq,q in enumerate(zeros): ...
      scores = A[:,None] + B[None,:]
      m = abs(r)
      if m < n:
        diff = np.abs(ones[:,None] - zeros[None,:])
        scores[diff == m] += 1
      # find best candidates with score < 0
      # To avoid huge memory? diff matrix alloc. For n=200, 100x100 10k, fine. For n=400 (200x200 40k) okay if limited.
      neg = np.argwhere(scores < 0).ravel()
      if neg.size:
        # sample with probability? Choose top maybe by scores values
        # flat idx to i,j
        if neg.size > 30:
           # choose lowest scores: get scores_flat = scores.ravel(); vals = scores_flat[neg]; use argpartition
           vals = scores.ravel()[neg]
           k = 30
           part = np.argpartition(vals, k)[:k]
           neg = neg[part]  # not sorted
        for f in neg:
           ip = f // zeros.size; iq = f % zeros.size
           cand.add((int(ones[ip]), int(zeros[iq])))
    if not cand:
       # random acceptance / restart
       # do 20 random swaps that do not increase max_count too much
       improved_any=False
       for _ in range(30):
          ip=rng.randint(ones.size); iq=rng.randint(zeros.size); p=ones[ip]; q=zeros[iq]
          delta=full_delta(h,p,q,n); new_count=(S+delta).max()
          if new_count <= max_count + 0.5: # accept plateau/slight
             S += delta; h[p]=0; h[q]=1; ones...; improved_any=True; break
       if not improved_any:
          # random restart: mutate best? break to outer run
          break
       no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 5: break
       continue
    # evaluate top unique candidates
    best_pair=None; best_new_count=max_count; best_delta=None
    for p,q in list(cand)[:200]:
       delta=full_delta(h,p,q,n); nc=(S+delta).max()
       if nc < best_new_count or (nc==best_new_count and p<best_pair): ...
    if best_new_count < max_count:
       S= S + best_delta? Need recompute delta since best_delta. Use stored.
       h[p]=0; h[q]=1
       if S.max() < best_count: best_count=S.max(); best_h=h.copy(); best_S=S.copy()
       no_improve=0
    else:
       # try random accept if close
       no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 8: break
  # ensure best exact sum
  return best_h, best_count*2/n

def full_delta(h,p,q,n):
  delta=np.zeros(2*n-1)
  hp_old=1? Could h values not exactly? For binary search, h should be 0/1. But after best_h maybe binary. Ensure.
  for idx in range(2*n-1):
    r = idx - (n-1)
    chg=0
    if r > 0:
      m=r
      if p < n-m: chg -= (1 - h[p+m])
      if p >= m: chg += h[p-m]
      if q < n-m: chg += (1 - h[q+m])
      if q >= m: chg -= h[q-m]
    elif r < 0:
      m=-r
      if p >= m: chg -= (1 - h[p-m])
      if p + m < n: chg += h[p+m]
      if q >= m: chg += (1 - h[q-m])
      if q + m < n: chg -= h[q+m]
      (r=0 no change because h(1-h) binary 0; but if due to fractional? For binary, no change? If p 1->0, term h(1-h) goes 0->0; q 0->1, 0->0, so 0.)
    if abs(p-q) < n:
       d=abs(p-q)
       if n-1-d >=0: delta[n-1-d] += 1
       if n-1+d < 2*n-1: delta[n-1+d] += 1
  return delta

`compute_all_c_own(h)`:
  n; c=np.empty(2n-1); dx
  c[n-1]=dx*np.sum(h*(1-h))
  for d in 1..n-1: L=n-d; c[n-1+d]=dx*np.sum(h[:L]*(1-h[d:])); c[n-1-d]=dx*np.sum(h[d:]*(1-h[:L]))
  return c
This is O(n^2) but in Python loop; for n=500, 500 loops * 500 =250k multiplications, negligible; but Python loop 500, okay. For 500, 500 loops, maybe 0.002s? Actually each dot vector 500, 500*500=250k C operations plus Python overhead 500, fine. For 1000, 1M+, okay. Use this for n≤800.

def subgrad_polish(h,n,deadline,dirs):
  best=eval_c5(h); h_best=h
  while time<deadline:
    c=compute_all_c_own(h); top_idx=np.argsort(c)[-3:]
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in top_idx: g += lag_grad(h, idx)
    g /= len(top_idx)

For current h, pick max lag r. Compute derivative g=lag_grad(h,r). Feasible coordinate move: find i with largest g_i (decreasing h_i reduces c?
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alpha,top_idx,maxiter):
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_con,'jac':_sum_con_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
      res=minimize(smooth_active_obj, h, jac=smooth_active_jac, args=(n,alpha,top_idx), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})
      return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
      return h.copy()
```

```
def outer_step(h,n,trust,top_m=20):
    c=compute_all_c_own(h); idx=tuple(np.argsort(c)[-top_m:])
    hvar=cp.Variable(n); tvar=cp.Variable()
    cons=[cp.sum(hvar)==n/2, hvar>=0, hvar<=1, cp.norm(hvar-h, ordInf)<=trust]
    for i in idx:
       g=lag_grad(h,i)
       cons.append(c[i] + g @ (hvar-h) <= tvar)
    prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(tvar), cons)
    for solver in (cp.GLOP,cp.CBC):
       try: prob.solve(solver=solver); break
       except: pass
    if prob.status in ...: ...
```
Use inf norm: `cp.norm(hvar - h, np.inf)`? CVXPY supports `cp.norm_inf`. Use `np.max(np.abs(hvar-h)) <= trust` maybe. `cp.abs` returns array, max. `cp.max(cp.abs(hvar - h)) <= trust`. Fine.

`smooth_active_obj` stable:
```
c = selected_c(x, top_idx)
m = c.max()
sh = alpha*(c-m)
mx=sh.max()
return float(m + (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx))))/alpha)
```
If all c equal, sh=0, mx=0, log(k), returns m+log(k)/alpha approximates max slightly above. Good.
Derivative weights = exp(sh-mx)/sum. Because gradient of m + (mx+logsumexp(shift-mx))/alpha = softmax grad. The `m` part? In derivative of logsumexp(c) with m max, m is one of c; if top element tie, derivative includes 1/k for max? Our formula using softmax over all c includes that. The objective m + logsumexp(alpha(c-m))/alpha derivative = ∑ softmax grad c. Good even with m.

`smooth_active_jac`:
```
c=selected_c; m=...; sh=alpha*(c-m); mx=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh-mx); w/=w.sum(); g=zeros; for t, idx: g += w[t]*lag_grad(x, int(idx)); return g
```
`lag_idx` expects idx int. Good.

Interpolation:
```
def interp_resize(h, n):
  if len(h)==n: return h.copy()
  x=np.linspace(0,2,len(h)); y=np.linspace(0,2,n)
  return np.interp(y,x,h)
```

```
def binary_milp(n, deadline, rng):
    h = cp.Variable(n, boolean=True)
    t = cp.Variable(nonneg=True)
    p = {}
    cons = [cp.sum(h) == n//2]
    for i in range(n):
       for j in range(i+1,n):
          # variable for pair
          pij = cp.Variable(boolean=True)
          p[(i,j)] = pij
          cons.append(pij <= h[i])
          cons.append(pij <= h[j])
          cons.append(pij >= h[i] + h[j] - 1)
    for d in range(1,n):
       L=n-d
       # positive lag: sum h[0:L] - sum p[i,i+d]
       expr = cp.sum(h[:L])
       for i in range(L): expr -= p[(i,i+d)]
       cons.append(t >= expr)
       # negative lag: sum h[d:n] - sum p[i,i+d]
       expr = cp.sum(h[d:])
       for i in range(L): expr -= p[(i,i+d)]
       cons.append(t >= expr)
    cons.append(t <= 1?) maybe upper bound to help: maximum S ≤ n? t <= n. Not necessary.
    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t), cons)
    solve...
```

MILP code size. Let's write compact.
```
def binary_milp(n, deadline, rng):
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem < 5: return None
    h = cp.Variable(n, boolean=True)
    t = cp.Variable(nonneg=True)
    p = {}
    cons = [cp.sum(h) == n//2]
    for i in range(n):
        hi = h[i]
        for j in range(i+1, n):
            pij = cp.Variable(boolean=True)
            p[(i,j)] = pij
            cons.append(pij <= hi)
            cons.append(pij <= h[j])
            cons.append(pij >= hi + h[j] - 1)
    for d in range(1,n):
        L = n-d
        expr_pos = cp.sum(h[:L])
        expr_neg = cp.sum(h[d:])
        for i in range(L):
            pij = p[(i,i+d)]
            expr_pos -= pij
            expr_neg -= pij
        cons.append(t >= expr_pos)
        cons.append(t >= expr_neg)
    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t), cons)
    tl = int(max(1, rem - 8))
    h_val = None; status=None
    for solver in [cp.CBC, cp.SCIP]:
        if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
        try:
            prob.solve(solver=solver, time_limit=tl, verbose=False)
            status = prob.status; h_val = h.value
            if status in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate') and h_val is not None: break
            # if timeout but h_val not None, could use and break? maybe continue? If one solver timed out, another maybe not. Use if h_val not None.
            if h_val is not None: break
        except Exception:
            continue
    if h_val is None: return None
    hb = np.asarray(h_val, dtype=float).round()
    return make_binary(hb, n) if abs(hb.sum()-n/2)>1e-6 else hb
```
Issue: For MIP, if status timeout but a feasible incumbent exists, `h.value` may be available. We can use. If not optimal but h_val non-None, break after first solver; maybe second solver could better, but time limited. Use first.

```
def _solve_mip(prob, solver, tl):
    try:
        prob.solve(solver=solver, time_limit=tl, verbose=False)
    except Exception:
        try:
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
        except Exception:
            pass
```

```
   c=eval_c5(x)
   return c + 100.0*(np.sum(x)-n/2.0)**2
```

```
def all_counts(h):
 n=len(h); c=np.empty(2*n-1); c[n-1]=np.sum(h*(1-h));
 for d in range(1,n):
   L=n-d; c[n-1+d]=np.dot(h[:L], 1-h[d:d+L]); c[n-1-d]=np.dot(h[d:], 1-h[:L])
 return c
```

```
 while time.time()<end-0.5:
   top_idx=np.argsort(S)[-3:]
   improved=False
   for idx in top_idx:
     g=lag_grad(h, idx)
     high=np.argsort(g)[-5:]; low=np.argsort(g)[:5]

     pairs.sort(key=lambda... no lambda! Need no lambdas. Use list of tuples and sort default by tuple (deriv,p,q). If deriv first, okay. Build `(gq-gp,int(p),int(q))`, sort.
     for deriv,p,q in pairs[:8]:

       # create candidates list without set allocation?
       opts=[]

            S += delta; h[p]-=d; h[q]+=d
```

`optimize_at_n` function:
```
def optimize_at_n(h_start,n,end,rng):
 h=project_feasible(...); S=all_counts(h)
 best_h,best_c = local_search(h,S,n,min(end-20, time+? maybe 40),rng)
 recompute S
 h,best_c = slsqp_active stages...
   after each, S=all_counts
   local_search short
   update best

```

SLSQP active implementation more compact:
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,end,rng):
 best_h=h; best_c=eval_c5(h)
 top_ms=[1,3,5,8]
 it=0
 while time.time() < end-5 and it<20:
   c=all_c(h)
   top=tuple(np.argsort(c)[-8:])
   improved=False
   for tm in top_ms:
     idxs=tuple(top[-tm:])
     if time.time()>end-5: break
     maxiter=1500 if n<200 else 800
     if tm==1:
        h_new=slsqp_single(h,n,idxs[0],maxiter)
     else:
        h_new=slsqp_smooth(h,n,20000,idxs,maxiter)
        if time... h_new=slsqp_smooth(...100000...)
     if eval(h_new)<best_c: update
     it+=1
   if not improved: break
 return best_h,best_c
```

```
  n=len(h); target=n/2.0; h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-15:
     # distribute to up to 100 entries
```

```
def binary_milp(n,end,rng):
 if time.time()>end-4: return None
 h=cp.Variable(n,boolean=True); t=cp.Variable(nonneg=True); p={}; cons=[cp.sum(h)==n//2]
 for i...:
   pi=cp.Variable(boolean=True); p[(i,j)]=pi; cons += [pi<=h[i], pi<=h[j], pi>=h[i]+h[j]-1]
 for d...:
   L=n-d; ep=cp.sum(h[:L]); en=cp.sum(h[d:])
   for i in range(L): pi=p[(i,i+d)]; ep-=pi; en-=pi
   cons.append(t>=ep); cons.append(t>=en)
 prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t), cons)
 tl=max(1,int(end-time.time()))
 try: prob.solve(solver=cp.CBC, time_limit=tl, verbose=False)
 except Exception:
   try: prob.solve(solver=cp.SCIP, time_limit=tl, verbose=False)
   except: return None
 val=h.value
 if val is None: return None
 val=np.round(np.asarray(val,float))
 if abs(val.sum()-n//2)>1e-6: val=make_binary(val,n)
 return val
```
This is ~35 lines. Good.

Potential issue: `cons += [list]` with cvxpy constraints; okay. Use `cons.extend` maybe.

MILP p dictionary with tuple keys; in t constraints retrieving. Fine.

```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,end,rng):
 best=...; S? no, use all_c.
 while time.time()<end-3:
   c=all_counts(h)*(2.0/n)
   top=np.argsort(c)
   improved=False
   for pos in [-1,-2,-3,-5,-8]:
      if len(top)<-pos: pos=-len(top)
      idxs=tuple(top[pos:])  # top pos? If pos=-1, last; if pos=-3 last3? In Python top[-3:] last 3, okay. But -8 maybe.
      maxiter=1200 if n>150 else 2000
      if len(idxs)==1: h_new=slsqp_single(h,n,idxs[0],maxiter)
      else:
         h_new=slsqp_smooth(h,n,5000,idxs,maxiter)
         if time.time()<end-3: h_new2=slsqp_smooth(h_new,n,50000,idxs,maxiter); h_new=h_new2
      cnew=eval_c5(h_new)
      if cnew<best_c-1e-14: h=h_new; best_c=cnew; improved=True
   if not improved: break
 return h,best_c
```

Now `interp_to`:
```
def interp_to(h,n):
 m=len(h)
 if m==n: return h.copy()
 x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m); y=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
 return np.interp(y,x,h)
```
`make_binary`:
```
def make_binary(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float)
 if len(h)!=n: h=interp_to(h,n)
 h=np.zeros(n); h[np.argsort(-h)[:n//2]]=1.0; return h
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 try: h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,float); n0=len(h0)
 except: h0=np.full(300,0.5); n0=300
 h0=project_feasible(h0,n0); best_c=eval_c5(h0); best_h=h0.copy()
 small_end=t0+min(280, budget_s*0.28)
 for n in (16,24,32,40):
   if time.time()>small_end-30: break
   h_start=None
   if time.time()<small_end-50:
      tmilp=min(time.time()+25, small_end-45)
      h_start=binary_milp(n, tmilp, rng)
   if h_start is None:
      h_start=interp_to(best_h,n)
   # DE for small
   if n<=32 and time.time()<small_end-35:
      h_de=de_search(n, min(time.time()+30, small_end-25), rng)
      if h_de is not None: h_start=h_de # maybe choose DE start; or compare quick? Use if de start eval better than interp?
   alloc=min(45, small_end-time.time()-4)
   if alloc>20:
      hb,cb=optimize_at_n(h_start,n,time.time()+alloc,rng)
```

Let's write `cont_compute_swap` carefully:
```
def cont_compute_swap(h, S, p, q, a, need_delta):
    n = len(h)
    m = len? no
    delta = np.zeros(2*n-1) if need_delta else None
    bestv = -1.0
    sq = a*a
    hp = h[p]; hq = h[q]
    off = n-1
    ddist = abs(p-q)
    for idx in range(2*n-1):
        r = idx - off
        d = 0.0
        if r > 0:
            m = r
            if p < n - m: d -= a * (1.0 - h[p + m])
            if p >= m: d += a * h[p - m]
            if q < n - m: d += a * (1.0 - h[q + m])
            if q >= m: d -= a * h[q - m]
            if ddist == m: d += sq
        elif r < 0:
            m = -r
            if p >= m: d -= a * (1.0 - h[p - m])
            if p + m < n: d += a * h[p + m]
            if q >= m: d += a * (1.0 - h[q - m])
            if q + m < n: d -= a * h[q + m]
            if ddist == m: d += sq
        else:
            d = 2.0 * a * (hp - hq) - 2.0 * sq
        if need_delta: delta[idx] = d
        v = S[idx] + d
        if v > bestv: bestv = v
    if need_delta: return bestv, delta
    return bestv
```
Is r=0 formula using hp,hq old; if p/q target? yes. If p and q same? no.

Wait for r=0, if moving a from p to q, and p maybe q? no. Good.

```
def local_search(h, S, n, end, rng):
    dx = 2.0/n
    h = h.copy(); S = S.copy()
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = eval_c5(h)
    accept_count=0; stall=0
    while time.time() < end - 0.5:
        top_idx = np.argsort(S)[-3:]
        improved=False
        for idx in top_idx:
            g = lag_grad(h, idx)
            order = np.argsort(g)
            low = order[:4]; high = order[-4:]
            pairs=[]
            for p in high:
                if h[p] <= 1e-8: continue
                for q in low:
                    if q == p or h[q] >= 1.0 - 1e-8: continue
                    pairs.append((g[q]-g[p], int(p), int(q)))
            pairs.sort()
            for deriv, p, q in pairs[:8]:
                maxd = float(min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q]))
                if maxd <= 1e-8: continue
                opts = [maxd, 0.5*maxd, 0.1*maxd]
                rr = idx - (n-1)
                if rr != 0 and abs(p-q) == abs(rr):
                    dopt = -0.5 * deriv
                    if dopt > 1e-8 and dopt < maxd: opts.append(float(dopt))
                accepted=False
                for a in opts:
                    if a <= 1e-8 or a >= maxd + 1e-12: # if a > maxd due dopt? dopt<maxd; opts maxd
                       if a > maxd + 1e-12: continue
                    nm = cont_compute_swap(h,S,p,q,float(a),False)
                    if nm * dx < best_c - 1e-14:
                        nm2, dl = cont_compute_swap(h,S,p,q,float(a),True)
                        S += dl; h[p] -= float(a); h[q] += float(a)
                        c = eval_c5(h)
                        if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                            best_c = c; best_h = h.copy(); best_S? no
                            accept_count +=1
                            improved=True
                            break
                        else:
                            S -= dl; h[p] += float(a); h[q] -= float(a)
                if improved: break
            if improved: break
        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if accept_count > 0 and accept_count % 25 == 0:
                S = all_counts(h)
                h = np.clip(h,0,1)
                # project? if drift, reproject
                h = project_feasible(h,n); S=all_counts(h)
        else:
            stall += 1
            # random plateau
            moved=False
            if stall <= 10 and time.time() < end - 1:
                ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 1e-8); zeros = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
                if len(ones) and len(zeros):
                    cur = S.max()
                    for _ in range(20):
                        p = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; q = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
                        maxd = float(min(h[p], 1.0-h[q])); a = maxd * rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0)
                        if a <= 1e-10: continue
                        nm = cont_compute_swap(h,S,p,q,a,False)
                        if nm <= cur + 1e-10:
                            nm2, dl = cont_compute_swap(..., True)
                            S += dl; h[p]-=a; h[q]+=a; h=np.clip(h,0,1); S=all_counts(h); moved=True; break
            if not moved and stall > 8:
                break
    return best_h, best_c
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
 target=0.5*n
 for _ in range(50):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
   if diff>0:
     room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
     if tot<=1e-14: break
     h += diff/tot*room
   else:
     tot=h.sum()
     if tot<=1e-14: break
     h += diff/tot*h
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-13:
   if diff>0:
      idxs=np.argsort(1-h)
      for i in idxs: 
         add=min(diff,1-h[i]); h[i]+=add; diff-=add
         if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
   else:
      idxs=np.argsort(h)
      for i in idxs:
         sub=min(-diff,h[i]); h[i]-=sub; diff+=sub
         if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
 return h
```
But `np.argsort(1-h)` creates array; okay. Since h clipped, if still diff due floating, may not fully adjust. Good.

Code:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(60):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0:
            idxs = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
            for i in idxs:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[i])
                if add <= 0:
                    continue
                h[i] += add
                diff -= add
                if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                    break
        else:
            idxs = np.argsort(h)
            for i in idxs:
                sub = min(-diff, h[i])
                if sub <= 0:
                    continue
                h[i] -= sub
                diff += sub
                if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                    break
    return h
```
Good.

`eval_c5`:
```
def eval_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))
```
If n=0 no. Good.

`all_counts`:
```
def all_counts(h):
    n=len(h); c=np.empty(2*n-1); c[n-1]=np.dot(h,1-h)
    for d in range(1,n):
        L=n-d
        c[n-1+d]=np.dot(h[:L], 1-h[d:d+L])
        c[n-1-d]=np.dot(h[d:], 1-h[:L])
    return c
```
If h has shape (n,), dot returns scalar. Good.

`selected_c`:
```
def selected_c(x, top_idx):
 n=len(x); dx=2.0/n; out=np.empty(len(top_idx))
 for t, idx in enumerate(top_idx):
  idx=int(idx); r=idx-(n-1)
  if r>0: L=n-r; out[t]=np.dot(x[:L],1-x[r:r+L])*dx
  elif r<0: m=-r; L=n-m; out[t]=np.dot(x[m:],1-x[:L])*dx
  else: out[t]=np.dot(x,1-x)*dx
 return out
```
Good.

`lag_grad`: scaled.
```
def lag_grad(x, idx):
 n=len(x); dx=2.0/n; g=np.zeros(n); idx=int(idx); r=idx-(n-1)
 if r>0:
   m=r; L=n-m
   g[:L] += 1.0 - x[m:m+L]
   g[m:] -= x[:L]
 elif r<0:
   m=-r; L=n-m
   g[:L] -= x[m:m+L]
   g[m:] += 1.0 - x[:L]
 else:
   g[:] = 1.0 - 2.0*x
 return g*dx
```
Good.

`_sum_con` etc.

`run_slsqp`:
```
def run_slsqp(obj, jac, x0, args, n, maxiter):
    h = project_feasible(x0, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _sum_con, 'jac': _sum_con_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(obj, h, jac=jac, args=args, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-14})
        if res.x is not None:
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h
```
Good.

```
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,top_idx):
 c=selected_c(x, top_idx); m=float(np.max(c)); sh=alpha*(c-m); mx=float(np.max(sh))
 val = m + float(mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - mx))))/alpha
 return float(val)
```
If alpha huge and sh-mx negative, exp okay. If top_idx length 0? no.

```
 def smooth_jac(...):
  c=...; sh; mx; w=np.exp(sh-mx); w /= w.sum(); grad=np.zeros(n)
  for k,idx in enumerate(top_idx): grad += w[k]*lag_grad(x,int(idx))
  return grad
```

`slsqp_polish`:

```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,end,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=eval_c5(h); best_h=h.copy()
    positions=(-1,-2,-3,-5,-8)
    stalls=0
    while time.time() < end - 3:
        c = all_counts(h) * (2.0 / n)
        order = np.argsort(c)
        improved = False
        for pos in positions:
            if time.time() > end - 5: break
            idxs = tuple(order[pos:])
            if len(idxs) == 0: continue
            maxit = 1200 if n > 180 else 2000
            if len(idxs) == 1:
                hnew = slsqp_single(h, n, idxs[0], maxit)
            else:
                hnew = slsqp_smooth(h, n, 5000.0, idxs, maxit)
                if time.time() < end - 5:
                    hnew = slsqp_smooth(hnew, n, 50000.0, idxs, maxit)
            cnew = eval_c5(hnew)
            if cnew < best_c - 1e-14:
                h = hnew; best_c = cnew; best_h = h.copy(); improved = True
        if not improved:
            stalls += 1
            if stalls >= 3: break
    return best_h, best_c
```
Potential: `order[pos:]` with pos=-5 if order length less returns all; good. `tuple(list of np.int64)`. Good.
Positions -1 repeated each cycle. Stalls break after 3 cycles if no improvement. Good.

```
def local_search(h, S, n, end, rng):
    h = h.copy(); S = S.copy()
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = eval_c5(h)
    accept = 0; stall = 0
    dx = 2.0 / n
    while time.time() < end - 0.5:
        top_idx = np.argsort(S)[-3:]
        improved = False
        for idx in top_idx:
            g = lag_grad(h, int(idx))
            order = np.argsort(g)
            low = order[:3]; high = order[-3:]
            pairs = []
            for p in high:
                if h[p] <= 1e-8: continue
                for q in low:
                    if q == p or h[q] >= 1.0 - 1e-8: continue
                    pairs.append((g[q] - g[p], int(p), int(q)))
            pairs.sort()
            for deriv, p, q in pairs[:6]:
                maxd = float(min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q]))
                if maxd <= 1e-8: continue
                opts = [maxd, 0.5 * maxd]
                rr = int(idx) - (n - 1)
                if rr != 0 and abs(p - q) == abs(rr):
                    dopt = -0.5 * deriv
                    if dopt > 1e-8 and dopt < maxd:
                        opts.append(float(dopt))
                for aa in opts:
                    if aa <= 1e-8 or aa > maxd + 1e-12:
                        continue
                    nm = cont_compute_swap(h, S, p, q, aa, False)
                    if nm * dx < best_c - 1e-14:
                        nm2, dl = cont_compute_swap(h, S, p, q, aa, True)
                        S = S + dl
                        h[p] -= aa; h[q] += aa
                        h = np.clip(h,0,1)
                        c = eval_c5(h)
                        if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                            best_c = c; best_h = h.copy(); accept += 1; improved = True
                            break
                        else:
                            S = S - dl; h[p] += aa; h[q] -= aa; h = np.clip(h,0,1)
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if accept > 0 and accept % 25 == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h, n); S = all_counts(h)
        else:
            stall += 1
            moved = False
            if stall <= 12 and time.time() < end - 1:
                ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 1e-8)
                zeros = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
                if len(ones) and len(zeros):
                    cur = float(S.max())
                    for _ in range(12):
                        p = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; q = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
                        maxd = float(min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q]))
                        aa = maxd * rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0)
                        if aa <= 1e-10: continue
                        nm = cont_compute_swap(h, S, int(p), int(q), aa, False)
                        if nm <= cur + 1e-10:
                            nm2, dl = cont_compute_swap(h, S, int(p), int(q), aa, True)
                            S = S + dl; h[p] -= aa; h[q] += aa; h = np.clip(h,0,1)
                            S = all_counts(h); h = project_feasible(h,n); S = all_counts(h)
                            c=eval_c5(h)
                            if c < best_c - 1e-14: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
                            moved = True
                            break
            if not moved and stall > 8:
                break
    return best_h, best_c
```
In random, `p` and `q` are np.int64; int() for cont. Good. After project, S recompute. Best_h maybe updated if eval improves. Good.
Potential if `low`/`high` as np arrays of dtype int, `p` np.int64, h[p] works. In pairs append int.

```
def optimize_at_n(h_start,n,end,rng):
    h=project_feasible(np.asarray(h_start,float),n); S=all_counts(h); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h)
    tlocal=min(end-25, time.time()+min(40, (end-time.time())*0.30))
    if tlocal > time.time()+3:
       h2,c2=local_search(h,S,n,tlocal,rng); h=h2; S=all_counts(h); if c2<best_c: update
    tslsqp=min(end-15, time.time()+min(80, (end-time.time())*0.45))
    if tslsqp > time.time()+3:
       h2,c2=slsqp_polish(h,n,tslsqp,rng); h=h2; S=all_counts(h); if c2<... update
    h2,c2=local_search(h,S,n,end-2,rng)
    if c2<best_c: update
    h=project_feasible(best_h,n); best_c=eval_c5(h)
    return h,best_c
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        h0=np.full(300,0.5)
    h0=project_feasible(h0,len(h0)); best_c=eval_c5(h0); best_h=h0.copy()
    small_end=t0+min(260, 0.28*budget_s)
    for n in (16,24,32,40):
        if time.time() > small_end - 35: break
        cands=[]
        if n <= 32 and time.time() < small_end - 45:
            hm=binary_milp(n, min(time.time()+20, small_end-40), rng)
            if hm is not None: cands.append(hm)
        cands.append(interp_to(best_h,n))
        if n <= 32 and time.time() < small_end - 30:
            hd=de_search(n, min(time.time()+20, small_end-25), rng)
            if hd is not None: cands.append(hd)
        cands.append(make_binary(interp_to(best_h,n),n))
        hstart=cands[0]; sc=eval_c5(project_feasible(hstart,n))
        for s in cands[1:]:
            sp=project_feasible(s,n); c=eval_c5(sp)
            if c < sc: sp? Actually hstart=sp
        alloc=min(50, small_end-time.time()-4)
        if alloc > 15:
            hb,cb=optimize_at_n(hstart,n,time.time()+alloc,rng)
            if cb < best_c: best_c=cb; best_h=hb.copy()
    main_end=deadline-20
    seen=set()
    for n in (len(best_h), 300, 360, 420):
        if n in seen or n < 4 or n > 1000: continue
        seen.add(n)
        if time.time() > main_end - 50: break
        hs = best_h if n == len(best_h) else interp_to(best_h,n)
        alloc=min(280, main_end-time.time()-30)
        if alloc > 35:
            hb,cb=optimize_at_n(hs,n,time.time()+alloc,rng)
            if cb < best_c: best_c=cb; best_h=hb.copy()
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
        hb,cb=optimize_at_n(best_h, len(best_h), deadline-2, rng)
        if cb < best_c: best_c=cb; best_h=hb.copy()
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1); target=.5*n
 for _ in range(50):
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
  if diff>0:
   room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
   if tot<=1e-14: break
   h+=diff/tot*room
  else:
   tot=h.sum()
   if tot<=1e-14: break
   h+=diff/tot*h
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-13:
  idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)) if diff>0 else int(np.argmax(h))
  h[idx]+=diff if diff>0 else diff  # but may violate bound if diff tiny? diff tiny.
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 return h
```

MILP reduce:
```
def binary_milp(n,end,rng):
 if time.time()>end-3: return None
 h=cp.Variable(n,boolean=True); t=cp.Variable(nonneg=True); p={}; cons=[cp.sum(h)==n//2]
 for i in range(n):
  hi=h[i]
  for j in range(i+1,n):
   pi=cp.Variable(boolean=True); p[(i,j)]=pi
   cons += [pi <= hi, pi <= h[j], pi >= hi + h[j] - 1]
 for d in range(1,n):
  L=n-d; ep=cp.sum(h[:L]); en=cp.sum(h[d:])
  for i in range(L):
   pi=p[(i,i+d)]; ep=ep-pi; en=en-pi
  cons += [t >= ep, t >= en]
 prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t),cons)
 try: prob.solve(solver=cp.CBC, time_limit=max(1,int(end-time.time())), verbose=False)
 except Exception: return None
 if h.value is None: return None
 val=np.round(np.asarray(h.value,float))
 if abs(val.sum()-n//2)>1e-6: val=make_binary(val,n)
 return val
```

```
 if n>40 or time.time()>end-8: return None
 kw=dict(popsize=8,maxiter=60 if n<=24 else 30,tol=1e-5,polish=False,seed=int(rng.randint(1,1000000)))
 try:
```

```
def local_search(h,S,n,end,rng):
 h=h.copy(); S=S.copy(); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h); accept=0; stall=0; dx=2./n
 while time.time()<end-.5:
  imp=False
  for idx in np.argsort(S)[-3:]:
   g=lag_grad(h,int(idx)); ordg=np.argsort(g); pairs=[]
   for p in ordg[-3:]:
    if h[p]<=1e-8: continue
    for q in ordg[:3]:
     if p!=q and h[q]<1-1e-8: pairs.append((g[q]-g[p],int(p),int(q)))
   pairs.sort()
   for deriv,p,q in pairs[:6]:
    md=float(min(h[p],1-h[q]))
    if md<=1e-8: continue
    opts=[md,.5*md]
    rr=int(idx)-(n-1)
    if rr and abs(p-q)==abs(rr):
     dp=-.5*deriv
     if 1e-8<dp<md: opts.append(dp)
    for a in opts:
     if a<=1e-8 or a>md+1e-12: continue
     if cont_compute_swap(h,S,p,q,a,False)*dx < best_c-1e-14:
      _,dl=cont_compute_swap(h,S,p,q,a,True); S=S+dl; h[p]-=a; h[q]+=a; h=np.clip(h,0,1); c=eval_c5(h)
      if c<best_c-1e-14:
       best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); accept+=1; imp=True; break
      else:
       S=S-dl; h[p]+=a; h[q]-=a; h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    if imp: break
   if imp: break
  if imp:
   stall=0
   if accept%25==0:
    h=project_feasible(h,n); S=all_counts(h)
  else:
   stall+=1; moved=False
   if stall<=10:
    ones=np.flatnonzero(h>1e-8); zeros=np.flatnonzero(h<1-1e-8)
    if len(ones) and len(zeros):
     cur=float(S.max())
     for _ in range(8):
      p=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; q=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; a=min(h[p],1-h[q])*rng.uniform(.1,1)
      if a>1e-10 and cont_compute_swap(h,S,int(p),int(q),a,False)<=cur+1e-10:
       _,dl=cont_compute_swap(h,S,int(p),int(q),a,True); S=S+dl; h[p]-=a; h[q]+=a
       h=project_feasible(h,n); S=all_counts(h); c=eval_c5(h)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
       moved=True; break
   if not moved and stall>7: break
 return best_h,best_c
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution
import cvxpy as cp

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(60):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h)) if diff > 0 else int(np.argmax(h))
        h[idx] += diff
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def eval_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def all_counts(h):
    n = len(h)
    c = np.empty(2 * n - 1)
    c[n - 1] = np.dot(h, 1.0 - h)
    for d in range(1, n):
        L = n - d
        c[n - 1 + d] = np.dot(h[:L], 1.0 - h[d:d + L])
        c[n - 1 - d] = np.dot(h[d:], 1.0 - h[:L])
    return c

def selected_c(x, top_idx):
    n = len(x)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    out = np.empty(len(top_idx))
    for t, idx in enumerate(top_idx):
        idx = int(idx)
        r = idx - (n - 1)
        if r > 0:
            L = n - r
            out[t] = np.dot(x[:L], 1.0 - x[r:r + L]) * dx
        elif r < 0:
            m = -r
            L = n - m
            out[t] = np.dot(x[m:], 1.0 - x[:L]) * dx
        else:
            out[t] = np.dot(x, 1.0 - x) * dx
    return out

def lag_grad(x, idx):
    n = len(x)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    g = np.zeros(n)
    idx = int(idx)
    r = idx - (n - 1)
    if r > 0:
        m = r
        L = n - m
        g[:L] += 1.0 - x[m:m + L]
        g[m:] -= x[:L]
    elif r < 0:
        m = -r
        L = n - m
        g[:L] -= x[m:m + L]
        g[m:] += 1.0 - x[:L]
    else:
        g[:] = 1.0 - 2.0 * x
    return g * dx

def _sum_con(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)

def _sum_con_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x)

def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha, top_idx):
    c = selected_c(x, top_idx)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    return float(m + (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - mx)))) / alpha)

def smooth_jac(x, n, alpha, top_idx):
    c = selected_c(x, top_idx)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    w = np.exp(sh - mx)
    w /= w.sum()
    g = np.zeros(n)
    for k, idx in enumerate(top_idx):
        g += w[k] * lag_grad(x, int(idx))
    return g

def slsqp_smooth(h, n, alpha, top_idx, maxiter):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _sum_con, 'jac': _sum_con_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(smooth_obj, h, jac=smooth_jac, args=(n, float(alpha), tuple(top_idx)),
                       method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-14})
        if res.x is not None:
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h

def slsqp_polish(h, n, end, rng):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(h)
    stalls = 0
    while time.time() < end - 3:
        order = np.argsort(all_counts(h) * (2.0 / n))
        improved = False
        for pos in (-1, -2, -3, -5, -8):
            if time.time() > end - 5:
                break
            idxs = tuple(order[pos:])
            maxit = 1200 if n > 180 else 2000
            alpha = 1.0 if len(idxs) == 1 else 5000.0
            hnew = slsqp_smooth(h, n, alpha, idxs, maxit)
            if len(idxs) > 1 and time.time() < end - 5:
                hnew = slsqp_smooth(hnew, n, 50000.0, idxs, maxit)
            cnew = eval_c5(hnew)
            if cnew < best_c - 1e-14:
                h = hnew
                best_c = cnew
                best_h = h.copy()
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            stalls += 1
            if stalls >= 3:
                break
    return best_h, best_c

def interp_to(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if len(h) == n:
        return h.copy()
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h))
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return np.interp(y, x, h)

def make_binary(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if len(h) != n:
        h = interp_to(h, n)
    out = np.zeros(n)
    out[np.argsort(-h)[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return out

def de_objective(x, n):
    return float(eval_c5(x) + 100.0 * (np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n) ** 2)

def de_search(n, end, rng):
    if n > 40 or time.time() > end - 8:
        return None
    try:
        res = differential_evolution(de_objective, [(0.0, 1.0)] * n, args=(n,),
                                     popsize=8, maxiter=60 if n <= 24 else 30,
                                     tol=1e-5, polish=False,
                                     seed=int(rng.randint(1, 1000000)))
        if res.x is not None:
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None

def binary_milp(n, end, rng):
    if time.time() > end - 3:
        return None
    h = cp.Variable(n, boolean=True)
    t = cp.Variable(nonneg=True)
    p = {}
    cons = [cp.sum(h) == n // 2]
    for i in range(n):
        hi = h[i]
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            pi = cp.Variable(boolean=True)
            p[(i, j)] = pi
            cons += [pi <= hi, pi <= h[j], pi >= hi + h[j] - 1]
    for d in range(1, n):
        L = n - d
        ep = cp.sum(h[:L])
        en = cp.sum(h[d:])
        for i in range(L):
            pi = p[(i, i + d)]
            ep = ep - pi
            en = en - pi
        cons += [t >= ep, t >= en]
    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t), cons)
    try:
        prob.solve(solver=cp.CBC, time_limit=max(1, int(end - time.time())), verbose=False)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if h.value is None:
        return None
    val = np.round(np.asarray(h.value, dtype=float))
    if abs(val.sum() - n // 2) > 1e-6:
        val = make_binary(val, n)
    return val

def cont_compute_swap(h, S, p, q, a, need_delta):
    n = len(h)
    delta = np.zeros(2 * n - 1) if need_delta else None
    bestv = -1.0
    sq = a * a
    hp = h[p]
    hq = h[q]
    off = n - 1
    dist = abs(p - q)
    for idx in range(2 * n - 1):
        r = idx - off
        d = 0.0
        if r > 0:
            m = r
            if p < n - m:
                d -= a * (1.0 - h[p + m])
            if p >= m:
                d += a * h[p - m]
            if q < n - m:
                d += a * (1.0 - h[q + m])
            if q >= m:
                d -= a * h[q - m]
            if dist == m:
                d += sq
        elif r < 0:
            m = -r
            if p >= m:
                d -= a * (1.0 - h[p - m])
            if p + m < n:
                d += a * h[p + m]
            if q >= m:
                d += a * (1.0 - h[q - m])
            if q + m < n:
                d -= a * h[q + m]
            if dist == m:
                d += sq
        else:
            d = 2.0 * a * (hp - hq) - 2.0 * sq
        if need_delta:
            delta[idx] = d
        v = S[idx] + d
        if v > bestv:
            bestv = v
    if need_delta:
        return bestv, delta
    return bestv

def local_search(h, S, n, end, rng):
    h = h.copy()
    S = S.copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(h)
    accept = 0
    stall = 0
    dx = 2.0 / n
    while time.time() < end - 0.5:
        imp = False
        for idx in np.argsort(S)[-3:]:
            g = lag_grad(h, int(idx))
            ordg = np.argsort(g)
            pairs = []
            for p in ordg[-3:]:
                if h[p] <= 1e-8:
                    continue
                for q in ordg[:3]:
                    if p != q and h[q] < 1.0 - 1e-8:
                        pairs.append((g[q] - g[p], int(p), int(q)))
            pairs.sort()
            for deriv, p, q in pairs[:6]:
                md = float(min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q]))
                if md <= 1e-8:
                    continue
                opts = [md, 0.5 * md]
                rr = int(idx) - (n - 1)
                if rr != 0 and abs(p - q) == abs(rr):
                    dp = -0.5 * deriv
                    if 1e-8 < dp < md:
                        opts.append(float(dp))
                for a in opts:
                    if a <= 1e-8 or a > md + 1e-12:
                        continue
                    if cont_compute_swap(h, S, p, q, a, False) * dx < best_c - 1e-14:
                        _, dl = cont_compute_swap(h, S, p, q, a, True)
                        S = S + dl
                        h[p] -= a
                        h[q] += a
                        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
                        c = eval_c5(h)
                        if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                            best_c = c
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            accept += 1
                            imp = True
                            break
                        else:
                            S = S - dl
                            h[p] += a
                            h[q] -= a
                            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
                if imp:
                    break
            if imp:
                break
        if imp:
            stall = 0
            if accept > 0 and accept % 25 == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                S = all_counts(h)
        else:
            stall += 1
            moved = False
            if stall <= 10 and time.time() < end - 1:
                ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 1e-8)
                zeros = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
                if len(ones) and len(zeros):
                    cur = float(S.max())
                    for _ in range(8):
                        p = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
                        q = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
                        a = float(min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q])) * float(rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0))
                        if a > 1e-10 and cont_compute_swap(h, S, int(p), int(q), a, False) <= cur + 1e-10:
                            _, dl = cont_compute_swap(h, S, int(p), int(q), a, True)
                            S = S + dl
                            h[p] -= a
                            h[q] += a
                            h = project_feasible(h, n)
                            S = all_counts(h)
                            c = eval_c5(h)
                            if c < best_c:
                                best_c = c
                                best_h = h.copy()
                            moved = True
                            break
            if not moved and stall > 7:
                break
    return best_h, best_c

def optimize_at_n(h_start, n, end, rng):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h_start, dtype=float), n)
    S = all_counts(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(h)
    t1 = min(end - 25, time.time() + min(40, (end - time.time()) * 0.30))
    if t1 > time.time() + 3:
        h2, c2 = local_search(h, S, n, t1, rng)
        h = h2
        S = all_counts(h)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h.copy()
    t2 = min(end - 15, time.time() + min(80, (end - time.time()) * 0.45))
    if t2 > time.time() + 3:
        h2, c2 = slsqp_polish(h, n, t2, rng)
        h = h2
        S = all_counts(h)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h.copy()
    h2, c2 = local_search(h, S, n, end - 2, rng)
    if c2 < best_c:
        best_c = c2
        best_h = h2.copy()
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 8
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(300, 0.5)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, len(h0))
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(h0)

    small_end = t0 + min(260, 0.28 * budget_s)
    for n in (16, 24, 32, 40):
        if time.time() > small_end - 35:
            break
        cands = []
        if n <= 32 and time.time() < small_end - 45:
            hm = binary_milp(n, min(time.time() + 20, small_end - 40), rng)
            if hm is not None:
                cands.append(hm)
        cands.append(interp_to(best_h, n))
        if n <= 32 and time.time() < small_end - 30:
            hd = de_search(n, min(time.time() + 20, small_end - 25), rng)
            if hd is not None:
                cands.append(hd)
        cands.append(make_binary(interp_to(best_h, n), n))
        hstart = project_feasible(cands[0], n)
        sc = eval_c5(hstart)
        for s in cands[1:]:
            sp = project_feasible(s, n)
            c = eval_c5(sp)
            if c < sc:
                sc = c
                hstart = sp
        alloc = min(50, small_end - time.time() - 4)
        if alloc > 15:
            hb, cb = optimize_at_n(hstart, n, time.time() + alloc, rng)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_c = cb
                best_h = hb.copy()

    main_end = deadline - 20
    seen = set()
    for n in (len(best_h), 300, 360, 420):
        if n in seen or n < 4 or n > 1000:
            continue
        seen.add(n)
        if time.time() > main_end - 50:
            break
        hs = best_h if n == len(best_h) else interp_to(best_h, n)
        base = 90 if n <= 32 else 280
        alloc = min(base, main_end - time.time() - 30)
        if alloc > 35:
            hb, cb = optimize_at_n(hs, n, time.time() + alloc, rng)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_c = cb
                best_h = hb.copy()

    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
        hb, cb = optimize_at_n(best_h, len(best_h), deadline - 2, rng)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_c = cb
            best_h = hb.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = float(eval_c5(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```
